# Citycoin.dev implementation report

This is the living completion report that master change order v5 (23 October 2026), section 43, requires. It was started on 24 October 2026. Every stage below updates it when its pull request merges, and again when its switch moves.

> **Status on 24 October 2026 (after the deploy of step 4; updated for stages S09 to S14):** seven stages are merged to main and deployed with every new switch off: S01 (PR #95, `e5d81ba`), S02, S02A and S07 (PR #96, `3810c69`), S03 and S04 (PR #97, `2608bd9`), and S08 (PR #98, `2639c50`). "Merged and deployed with its switches off" means the code is on the live site and changes nothing there; it is not "activated". The live site is main `2639c50` (deploy run 91, finished 15:40:58 UTC) and behaves as `4aa408e` did: the checks after runs 89 and 90 found the pages and APIs unchanged, and after run 90 the redirects too, and S08 left the Worker bundle and the built pages byte-identical (section 11). The one addition is the static files under `/brand/` from PR #95, which no page links to (section 2). At the owner's request all member data was deleted from the production D1 at 16:56 UTC on 24 October 2026, so production has had 0 members since then (section 2). Nothing that master v5 requires is activated, audited or on mainnet. No Cloudflare, DNS or other provider setting was changed, and citycoin.dev is not attached. Stages S09 to S14 (wallet-only sign-in, registration, the connect page, location once and the legacy link window) are this pull request (not merged). They are built behind `WALLET_ONLY` and `LEGACY_LINK`, which stay unset: with them unset the site keeps today's answers and pages, apart from three things (section 11): the bytes of seven page scripts that take today's paths, two new static files that no page links to, and forged small-order Ed25519 signatures, which today's verify can accept and which are refused on every signed route whatever the switches (review fix SEC-2, `src/solana.js` `verifySignature`); honest wallets are unaffected. Turning them on is S15, a later configuration pull request. The draft custom program `vicinity_curve` is PR #83 (branch `feat/vicinity-curve`, cited at `ec148c2`), open and not merged; it is being renamed to Citycoin names on a separate branch before the outside audit, and is not deployed anywhere public. All four release statuses in section 1 are "not done". Two of the 50 acceptance checks (B02 and D09) already hold today. They hold because of existing code and unchanged identifiers, not because of any v5 work; D09 holds only for its legacy half since owner amendment 01 (section 7). Section 7 gives the evidence for both.
>
> **Owner amendment 01 (24 October 2026):** the main token becomes a new Solana mint supplied by the owner, configured in one place (`MAIN_TOKEN_MINT`) and verified on chain before use; the new official X and Telegram addresses are configured the same way (`OFFICIAL_X_URL`, `OFFICIAL_TELEGRAM_URL`). `$VICINITY` becomes a legacy token, and nobody's holdings are swapped or migrated. The owner supplied the X address on 24 October 2026 (below); the mint and the Telegram address are not supplied yet and stay empty. Stage S02A built the central configuration (merged in PR #96 with its switch off); external dependency E18 is the owner's verified values. Details: `amendment-01-new-main-token.md`.
>
> **Owner decisions of 24 October 2026 (in writing):** (1) The official X account is `@CityCoinDev`: `OFFICIAL_X_URL` = `https://x.com/CityCoinDev` in `wrangler.jsonc`, set by the configuration pull request from branch `cc/config-x` (not merged). With every master switch off it is shown nowhere public; only the admin configuration view reports that it is set. Its verification record is incomplete: the account was read only by an automated HTTP read (no person has opened it in a browser yet), and nothing yet proves it is the owner's (`amendment-01-new-main-token.md` section 4.4). (2) No forwarding from `vicinity.city`, `www.vicinity.city`, `vicinitycity.com` or `www.vicinitycity.com` to citycoin.dev; after the move both names are left blank for the owner's other use. Stage S56 (the 308 redirects) is dropped. S55 makes citycoin.dev canonical and removes the vicinity.city routes with no redirect, after the legacy launch path is closed (S58); the vicinitycity.com forward, which comes from the countdown Worker `vicinity-countdown` and not from this Worker, ends when the owner detaches that domain (E16). Details: `domain-cutover.md` section 0. Nothing of this is applied.

### How to read this report

- **"Today"** means the live site. Since deploy run 91 (24 Oct 2026, 15:40:58 UTC) it is main `2639c50`, and it behaves as `4aa408e` did because every new switch is unset or off (S08, the last stage merged, adds no call site in the Worker). Its member data was deleted at the owner's request at 16:56 UTC the same day (section 2). The report was started at `4aa408e` (branch `claude/gifted-hamilton-cl6bct`, no tracked file changed then). Its `path:line` citations are at `4aa408e` unless a row names another revision; the merged stages moved some of those lines, and with their switches off the behavior cited is unchanged. This update is written on local branch `cc/s09r`: `2639c50` with the S09 to S14 code of this pull request applied on top, every code and test file byte-identical to the reviewed head `6541725` of branch `cc/s09-reviewed` (five commits on `c9798d2`: `8963e0b` S09 and S10, `c6fd7b6` S11 and S13, `dee1569` S12, `b1a986f` S14 and `6541725` the review fixes; the rows below call that branch `cc/s09`). Paths without a prefix are relative to the repository root. `curve:` paths point into the draft program checkout at `ec148c2`, relative to its `solana/` folder.
- Every statement about the current system cites `path:line` or a command listed in section 12. The deletion of all member data at the owner's request (24 Oct 2026, 16:56 UTC) and the read-only count after it are listed in section 12 with the statements used. The inputs were five working reconciliation notes covering identity, selection and roles, metadata and launch, programs and money, and brand, domain and UI. They are working files outside the repository, and their content is consolidated in `00-reconciliation.md`, `rebrand-inventory.md` and `schema-and-api.md`. Every fact this report takes from them was re-checked in code or by a command run for this report.
- **Stage** means one pull request (steps 2 and 3 each carried more than one stage, PR #96 and PR #97, and this pull request carries six, S09 to S14). **Switch** means a `wrangler.jsonc` variable read like today's switches in `src/flags.js`. Changing a switch is its own pull request, following the repository's existing rule (wrangler.jsonc:35, 38). **Closes** names the acceptance checks whose full pass condition is verified at that stage. **Advances** names checks that the stage builds part of.
- This report contains no estimates of time or cost.

## 5. Switches (none set in production)

Today's switches are the ones in `src/flags.js`. None of the switches below existed at `4aa408e`. The merged stages built `BRAND`, `SITE_ORIGIN` and `SITE_ORIGINS` (S02), `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` with its three values (S02A), `DEPLOYMENT_ENV` and `PUBLIC_V5` (S07) and `HOST_REDIRECT` (S03); S04's host binding has no switch of its own. Each of them is unset or off in production, and `wrangler.jsonc` sets none of them, so the live site behaves as `4aa408e` did. The one exception is a value, not a switch: the configuration pull request from branch `cc/config-x` (not merged) sets `OFFICIAL_X_URL` to the X address the owner supplied on 24 Oct 2026; with `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` off it is shown nowhere public, and only the admin configuration view (`GET /api/admin/config`) reports that it is set, never its value. This pull request (S09 to S14, not merged) builds `WALLET_ONLY`, `LEGACY_LINK` and `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL`; all three are unset in production, and `wrangler.jsonc` sets none of them. The other names of `src/masterpolicy.js` `FLAG_VALUES` are only checked by the S07 configuration check, and no feature reads them yet. S08 (merged in PR #98) maps six of them (`SELECTION_V5`, `ACTIVITY_POINTS`, `MANAGER_APPS`, `METADATA_V5`, `LAUNCH_V5`, `CC_HOURLY`) to schema groups in `groupsForFlags`. No Worker route or job reaches it: only the runner's gate, which no Worker path calls, and the operator backup tool use it. `DASHBOARD_V5`, `INDEXING` and the theme switch do not exist in code. Each default reproduces today's behavior exactly. The names follow `schema-and-api.md` section 2.2, which also lists each switch's named validation errors. The last block comes from the brand, domain and UI reconciliation. S22 (this pull request, not merged) adds no switch: no route, job or page reaches its two modules, and `METADATA_V5` (S24) stays the first switch of the metadata work. S39 (this pull request, not merged) adds no switch either: the swap platform fee is pinned to 0, and the two old settings `SWAP_PLATFORM_FEE_BPS` and `SWAP_FEE_ACCOUNT` are read only to be refused (any value but unset, blank or "0", and any fee account, stops the swap quotes and builds; section 10). S23 (this pull request, not merged) builds `LEGACY_LAUNCH`, which is unset in production and which `wrangler.jsonc` does not set, and deletes `COIN_V2_BATCH`, a switch from before v5 that was never on; a value left for it in the Worker's settings changes nothing (the two rows below).

| Switch | Values (default first) | Turns on |
|---|---|---|
| `SITE_ORIGIN`, `SITE_ORIGINS` | today's hosts | Canonical origin and the allowlist of accepted hosts (configuration, not a feature switch; adapter built in S02, merged in PR #96; both unset in production). Since S04 (merged in PR #97, `2608bd9`) `SITE_ORIGINS` listing a host other than vicinity.city (or a set value that `src/site-config.js` refuses) also turns on the session and challenge host binding (`src/hostbind.js`, `domain-cutover.md` section 1.2); unset, nothing changes. **S06 (this pull request, not merged; owner's go of 25 Oct 2026):** `wrangler.jsonc` sets `SITE_ORIGINS` = `https://citycoin.dev https://vicinity.city`, in the same file that attaches citycoin.dev; `SITE_ORIGIN` stays unset (vicinity.city canonical until S55). Once deployed the binding counts: members' existing sessions (host NULL) stay valid on vicinity.city, new sessions, pairings, hand-offs and sign-ups record their host, a host outside the allowlist (www.citycoin.dev included) answers 421 `wrong_host`, and (S06 review fix) the code e-mail and the wallet and Google notices name the host the request was served as (`src/hostbind.js` `mailOrigin`; while the binding does not count, today's vicinity.city). Still unset in production until that deploy. **Go-live (this pull request, branch `cc/golive`, not merged; owner's go of 25 Oct 2026):** `SITE_ORIGIN` = `https://citycoin.dev`: citycoin.dev becomes the canonical origin while vicinity.city still serves in place (`SITE_ORIGINS` unchanged). Links the Worker builds from the configuration (the social image of the pages, the websites list, new coins' metadata, mail buttons with no served host) name citycoin.dev. The old names' 301 keeps its constant target, vicinity.city. Rollback: `""`. |
| `DEPLOYMENT_ENV` | unset, test, preview, production | The environment the v5 configuration check validates the switches against (configuration, not a feature switch; added in S07, a delegated name; merged in PR #96, unset in production). A deployment that changes any v5 switch must set it (`ENVIRONMENT_UNSET`). **S06 (this pull request, not merged):** `wrangler.jsonc` sets `production`, which also turns off the local development exemption of the host binding on the production Worker (`src/site-config.js` `localDevAllowed`). Consequences recorded by the S06 tests and review: with it (and `SITE_ORIGINS`) set, a switch whose only missing inputs were the environment and the site would count if set alone, in `wrangler.jsonc` or as a dashboard variable kept by `keep_vars`. Computed one switch at a time on the 5150529 and S06 settings: **`WALLET_ONLY=on` goes from held (every sign-in 503) to fully live** (Google, e-mail, password and tiny-transfer sign-in 404 for everyone, only the wallet challenge left); `PUBLIC_V5=on` counts; `LEGACY_LAUNCH=off` is accepted; `ACTIVITY_POINTS=shadow` opens the cc5-activity gate; `LEGACY_SEATS=frozen` is accepted by the check but stays held until the pages are built for it; `HOST_REDIRECT=on` stays refused. With no switch set every gate is the same on both settings. Hence item 3 of the S06 deploy checklist (section 11). `wrangler dev` reads the same value and presents local visits as `http://citycoin.dev` (the first route), served as `https://citycoin.dev`: reads work, a local browser's writes answer 403 `wrong_origin`; a local run sets `DEPLOYMENT_ENV=test` in its git-ignored `.dev.vars` (`.dev.vars.example`) and uses `--local-upstream localhost:8787` (README). |
| `HOST_REDIRECT` | off, on | 308 from old public GET and HEAD pages to citycoin.dev. Built in S03 (`src/hostroute.js`, merged in PR #97, `2608bd9`; unset in production): it counts only through `src/v5policy.js` `hostRedirect`, while the deployed `wrangler.jsonc` sends every page through the Worker (`run_worker_first: true`, recorded by the page build in `src/pages-built.js`), `SITE_ORIGIN` and `SITE_ORIGINS` are accepted, and the v5 check accepts the deployment (`DEPLOYMENT_ENV` set, `SITE_ORIGIN=https://citycoin.dev`). Refusals: `HOST_REDIRECT_PAGES_BYPASS_WORKER`, `HOST_REDIRECT_SITE_REFUSED`, `HOST_REDIRECT_POLICY_REFUSED`, each logged once. **Not to be turned on:** the owner decided on 24 Oct 2026 that the old names are not forwarded (section 10, `domain-cutover.md` section 0), so S56 is dropped and the switch stays unset; a later cleanup stage removes the code (S65). |
| `WALLET_ONLY` | off, on | Wallet-only sign-in and registration; location once. Sign-in built in S09 and S10 (branch `cc/s09`; this pull request, not merged; unset in production): it counts only through `src/v5policy.js` `walletOnly`, while the v5 check accepts the switch's own inputs (`DEPLOYMENT_ENV`, `SITE_ORIGIN` and `SITE_ORIGINS`, which the switch requires by name) and `src/site-config.js` accepts the origins. Since the S09-S14 review an error in an unrelated input (an `OFFICIAL_X_URL` typo, a refused `MAIN_TOKEN_MINT`, a malformed `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL`) switches off only its own feature, never wallet-only sign-in. Refusals: `WALLET_ONLY_SITE_REFUSED`, `WALLET_ONLY_POLICY_REFUSED`, each logged once per isolate as `wallet-only switch refused <code>`. Set but refused, the Worker fails closed (`walletOnlyHeld`, review finding SEC-1): only wallet-bound sessions count, the Google, e-mail, password, tiny-transfer and unlink routes stay the unknown-path 404, every sign-in, registration and pairing route answers 503 `unavailable`, and `/api/me` says `walletOnly: true`, until the configuration is fixed. Only `WALLET_ONLY` unset or `off` is today's site (apart from the refusal of forged small-order signatures, which no switch governs: review fix SEC-2, S09 row). The page build stops on a `WALLET_ONLY=on` in `wrangler.jsonc` that the Worker would refuse, and on any other error the v5 check names there. Registration (S11) and location once (S13) are built server side in this pull request, behind the same gate. The connect page, the dashboard controls, the wallet account-change listener and the admin page's challenge (S12) are built in this pull request; the pages read the switch only through `/api/me` (`walletOnly: true`), so no page build changes with it. The legacy link window (S14) is built in this pull request, behind `LEGACY_LINK`; the switch stays off until S15. **Go-live (this pull request, branch `cc/golive`, not merged; owner's go of 25 Oct 2026):** `WALLET_ONLY` = `on`. It counts with the committed `DEPLOYMENT_ENV`, `SITE_ORIGIN` and `SITE_ORIGINS`, and the Google, e-mail, password, tiny-transfer and unlink routes answer 404. The pages are built with the wallet-only copy (`scripts/pages/walletonly-copy.mjs`, recorded as `walletOnly: true` in `src/pages-built.js`). The page build refuses the switch without `BRAND=citycoin`, and `BRAND=citycoin` without it. Rollback: `"off"`. |
| `LEGACY_LINK` + `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL` | off, on | Time-boxed wallet-link window for Google- or email-only accounts. Built in S14 (branch `cc/s09`; this pull request, not merged; both unset in production): it counts only through `src/v5policy.js` `legacyLink`, which needs `WALLET_ONLY` to count and the v5 check to accept its own inputs (`MISSING_LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL`, `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL_INVALID`). The window is open while `LEGACY_LINK` is on and `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL` is still ahead, and closed (410) once `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL` has passed while `LEGACY_LINK` is still on. Since the S09-S14 review (SEC-4) `LEGACY_LINK` off is no window at all, whatever `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL` says (the S10 404 under `WALLET_ONLY`), and the scheduled job marks unlinked accounts `closed_unresolved` only after a window was recorded open (the `settings` row `legacy_window_open`, written when the window is used) and that recorded end has passed; a later window reopens a `closed_unresolved` account. A restricted `legacy_link` session row never counts as a full session, whatever the switches say (SEC-1). Refusals of `LEGACY_LINK=on`: `LEGACY_LINK_WALLET_ONLY_OFF`, `LEGACY_LINK_POLICY_REFUSED`, `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL_PASSED`, each logged once per isolate as `legacy link switch refused <code>`. The page build stops when `wrangler.jsonc` sets `LEGACY_LINK=on` with any of them, and on a past `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL` whatever `LEGACY_LINK` says. **Go-live (this pull request, branch `cc/golive`, not merged; owner's go of 25 Oct 2026):** both stay unset only if the pre-merge census finds no Google- or e-mail-only account (go-live deploy checklist item 3). Production had 0 members after the deletion of 24 Oct 2026, and vicinity.city has kept Google and e-mail sign-up open since then. If the census finds any, the go-live pull request sets the window, or the owner closes those accounts in writing. |
| `LEGACY_SEATS` | on, frozen | "frozen": every seat, squad, endorsement, objection, resign and election writer answers 410. Built server side in S16 (local branch `cc/s16`, not merged; unset in production): it counts only through `src/v5policy.js` `legacySeats`, which answers "frozen" while the v5 check accepts the switch's OWN inputs (`DEPLOYMENT_ENV`; `LEGACY_SEATS_CODES`) or while `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` counts (one gate for both freezes). An error in an unrelated input (an `OFFICIAL_X_URL` typo, a refused `MAIN_TOKEN_MINT`, any other switch's input) never unfreezes it. Set to "frozen" but refused (`DEPLOYMENT_ENV` unset or unknown), the freeze HOLDS ("held": master section 15, "do not let historical rules authorize new actions"), logged once per isolate as `legacy seats switch refused LEGACY_SEATS_POLICY_REFUSED (held frozen)`. A value outside the set ("off", "true", "freeze", a typo) is refused by name (`FLAG_VALUE_INVALID`) and, since the S16 review fixes (SEC-S16-3), HOLDS the freeze too, logged once per isolate as `legacy seats switch refused LEGACY_SEATS_VALUE_REFUSED (held frozen)`: a Cloudflare dashboard typo never reopens the writers (only unset, blank and "on" are today's site; "off" is not). While frozen: the writers answer 410 `seats_frozen` before any read or write (the session included; the test lab's reset is one of them since review fix SEC-S16-5), the job's seats and elections steps are skipped, the balance samples go on but update no hold streak, a founder seat alone no longer moderates (`src/roles.js` `moderatesCity`), and `/api/policy` (key `legacySeats`), `/api/me` (key `legacySeats`, no founder path, endorsement, vote or squad offered, no Founder-ready badge) and `/api/moderator` stop describing the old rules as current. Since the S16 review fixes: a founder or manager action below the seat's amount is refused with 403 `below_amount` and writes nothing (`src/seats.js` `stillFounder`: no grace period starts and no seat is released, S16-R1 / SEC-S16-1), a founder coin action needs the seat's own wallet on the account (`src/coins.js`, `src/lplaunch.js`; SEC-S16-4), and a seat, an open application or a squad place no longer blocks an unlink (`src/walletlink.js`; SEC-S16-2). The history reads stay, and nobody loses a seat or a term. The page copy follows the same switch at build time (S16 copy part, same local branch): the page build reads `LEGACY_SEATS` from `wrangler.jsonc` only (never from the shell), and with `frozen` there it applies `scripts/pages/frozen-copy.mjs` to the home, rules, cities, token, connect and dashboard pages and records `legacySeats: "frozen"` in `src/pages-built.js`; the build stops when `LEGACY_SEATS=frozen` is set without an accepted `DEPLOYMENT_ENV`. The Worker counts the freeze as "frozen" only when the setting and the page record agree; when they differ the freeze still HOLDS, logged once per isolate as `legacy seats switch refused LEGACY_SEATS_PAGES_NOT_BUILT (held frozen)` (the setting without rebuilt pages: today's static copy stays until the rebuild, while the dashboard and map scripts already offer no seat action) or `LEGACY_SEATS_UNSET_PAGES_FROZEN (held frozen)` (pages built frozen, setting gone). While frozen, `/api/rank` drops `founderMin` and `/api/seats` and `/api/claims` drop `founderAmount`, each under its own cache key (`/api/verify` carries no ladder figure), and the wallet badge no longer promises a founder path. With `LEGACY_SEATS` unset or `on`, the built pages and `src/pages-built.js` are byte-identical to `c5df86a`. **Go-live (this pull request, branch `cc/golive`, not merged; owner's go of 25 Oct 2026):** `LEGACY_SEATS` = `frozen`, with the pages built frozen. Rollback: `"on"` (never a deleted line). |
| `SELECTION_V5` | off, shadow, on | Six-hour snapshot engine; "shadow" writes dry-run cycles only |
| `FOUNDER_RECORD_CHAIN` | off, on | Reads and mirrors on-chain FounderRecords |
| `ACTIVITY_POINTS` | off, shadow, on | Activity ledger |
| `MANAGER_APPS` | off, on | Country-manager applications and the owner inbox |
| `METADATA_V5` | off, on | Protected defaults, founder requests, owner metadata inbox |
| `LAUNCH_V5` | off, on | Self-service launch on the custom program (one reconciliation note called this `CC_LAUNCH`) |
| `LEGACY_LAUNCH` | on, off | "off": the website builds or accepts no new legacy approval, launch or coin design; it still records a coin the chain already has. Built in S23 (local branch `cc/s23`; this pull request, not merged; unset in production until S58, now until the go-live pull request, PR #106, is deployed, which sets it to `off`). It counts only through `src/v5policy.js` `legacyLaunch`, which answers "off" while the v5 check accepts the switch's OWN inputs (`DEPLOYMENT_ENV`; `LEGACY_LAUNCH_CODES`), in the pattern of `LEGACY_SEATS` and `WALLET_ONLY`. An error in an unrelated input (an `OFFICIAL_X_URL` typo, a refused `MAIN_TOKEN_MINT`, `LAUNCH_V5` refused for `LAUNCH_V5_WITH_LEGACY_LAUNCH`, any other switch's input) neither closes the path nor reopens it. Set to "off" but refused (`DEPLOYMENT_ENV` unset or unknown), or set to a value outside the set ("of", "false", "closed", a typo), the path is HELD closed, logged once per isolate as `legacy launch switch refused LEGACY_LAUNCH_POLICY_REFUSED (held off)` or `legacy launch switch refused LEGACY_LAUNCH_VALUE_REFUSED (held off)`, next to the v5 check's own `policy config refused` line. The direction comes from the master: a value that is not "on" is never a reason to keep building launches on the program the master retires: "Keep historical records readable, but do not let historical rules authorize new actions" (master-v5.txt line 924, section 15), "Keep a narrow legacy compatibility module only if existing assets require it; prevent new launches from using that route" (lines 1566-1567, section 27), "Coordinate legacy new-launch disable plus cross-program duplicate checks before allowing new launches" (lines 2099-2100, section 37 step 9), and "Runtime validation rejects incompatible flags and missing required deployment inputs with named errors" (line 982, section 16). Closing ends no coin and touches nothing launched. Only unset, blank and "on" are today's site. While closed (`src/legacylaunch.js`), every legacy launch writer and builder answers 410 `legacy_launch_closed` ("New city-coin launches on this launchpad are closed. Coins already launched keep trading. Nothing was changed.", `Cache-Control: no-store`), the S16 freeze's shape for a retired route. It answers before any session, database, chain or body read beyond the one field that names the write, first from `src/index.js` and again inside the module. Because it comes before the sign-in check, a signed-out or non-owner caller gets 410 instead of 401 or 403 (for example a member's `POST /api/admin/lp/approve/tx`: 403 with the path open, 410 while closed; review note PAR-2); it says only that the path is closed, which `/api/me` tells everyone anyway. The writers: the founder's launch (`POST /api/lp/launch/tx`); the founder studio (`POST /api/coins/design`: name, description, pair, colour, logo); the old LaunchLab contract (`POST /api/coins/mint`) and its approval (`/api/coins/mint/decide` with `approve: true`; a rejection still settles the city); the owner's approval (`POST /api/admin/lp/approve/tx`); the three setup steps that serve only new launches (`/api/admin/lp/tx` with step start, config or allow) and the records of those and of an approval (`/api/admin/lp/record` with step approve, start, config or allow); resuming launches (`/api/admin/lp/pause/tx` with `launches: false`); and, where their switches are on, the ready-made coin (`/api/coins/ready/art` and `/use`), every coin-review POST except cancel, and a reviewer's approval. Since the review fixes, three writes stay open because they only close something (findings CL-1, S23-OFF-2 and SEC-S23-3): the founder's cancel of their own coin request (`POST /api/coins/review/cancel`; `REVIEW_OPEN`), a reviewer's "ask for changes" (`/api/admin/coins/reviews/decide` with exactly `action: "changes_needed"`; `decisionOpen`; any other body is refused), and the founder taking their own picture down (`POST /api/coins/design` with exactly `{ "removeLogo": true }`; `logoRemovalOnly`; `src/coins.js` `removeOwnLogo` changes nothing else and keeps the studio's rules: the founder's session, no launched or approved design, the daily edit count, the public log line "logo removed"). So nothing waits for ever in the queue. The relay (`POST /api/swap/send`, `src/relay.js`) refuses with the same 410 a transaction that carries a vicinity_launchpad `approve_launch` or `launch` instruction, matched on the live program's id only (`closedLaunchTx`; finding SEC-S23-4): such a transaction built just before the switch closed (its relay ticket lasts 10 minutes) is not sent for it afterwards, though the signer could still send the same bytes to any public node. A route that is not switched on here keeps its own 404 first (the launchpad settings off, `COIN_V2_READY` or `COIN_V2_REVIEW` off). Not closed: every GET, the revoke of an approval and its record, pausing launches (S58 starts with it), the hand-over to the Squads vault, the fee accounts, the speed table, the partner entry, fee collection, holder rewards, forwarding, platform fees, graduation, the founder's fee collection, the rejection of a waiting LaunchLab contract, the three closing writes above, takedowns, the job, and the launch record (`POST /api/lp/record`, `GET /api/lp/coin` and the job's `lpCoins` sweep record a coin the chain already has, so one city keeps one coin). The switch builds nothing and pauses nothing on chain: a founder holding an unexpired Approval can still launch by calling vicinity_launchpad directly, and the website then records and lists that coin. Only the owner's `set_pause(launches = true)` plus revoking every pending Approval stops launches on chain, and a held value closes only the website's side (findings S23-DOC-1, SEC-S23-1). The launch status says `state: "closed"` with the neutral line for a city with no coin, and is unchanged for a launched one; the ready-made coin answers the `legacy_launch_closed` gate (or `launched`), and its review card is read-only history (`review.closed: true`: the founder's request on record with words that promise no launch, Cancel for their own open request, nothing that builds toward an approval, no launch hold and no screening to act on; finding S23-OFF-4). The job's ticker notice says the neutral line instead of a launch (S23-OFF-5, SEC-S23-2). `/api/me`, the admin probe `GET /api/admin/lp` and `/api/official` carry `legacyLaunch: "off"` (closed or held), and the pages read the switch only there; the owner's candidates list says the closed line first, so no candidate is approvable (S23-OFF-6). Admin, City coins: the neutral line once per section instead of the buttons of setup steps 1, 3 and 4, of the approval cards and of Resume; the coin designs on record listed without an Approve button and without the line on each card; "Approvals on record"; no "then approve cities" in the intro or in step 8 (S23-OFF-3, CL-2). Moderation, Coin reviews: "Ask for changes" only, with one line saying why, and plain words for a 410 (S23-OFF-2). The founder's launch card: "Closed" with the line, and an approval still on chain shown as on record with its date (S23-OFF-4). The dashboard: no coin studio and no "Design … coin" anywhere (the role card's jump and lead, also with the seats frozen; the City tab's line; the coin card's kicker, status and pair; the trade card's link and note), no "Add your coin's contract" item, the Founder card's coin fact "Closed" or "Approved (closed)", the closed line once in the Founder tab, and a "Take my picture down" link under a founder's own picture (S23-OFF-1, CL-2, SEC-S23-3). The Launchpad page's Upcoming note: "Designs on record, not launched." (S23-OFF-6). No page build reads the switch: the build's v5 check already stops on a refused value in `wrangler.jsonc` (`ENVIRONMENT_UNSET`, `FLAG_VALUE_INVALID`), and with the switch unset the built pages and `src/pages-built.js` are byte-identical to `37e5fbf`. One deliberate exception to "set but not readable as on is closed": a setting that cannot be read at all (reading it throws, which plain Worker variables cannot do) counts as unset, as for `LEGACY_SEATS`, so a platform fault never switches launches off silently; `launchClosed` itself has no catch of its own any more (S23-NIT-1, SEC-S23-5). **Go-live (this pull request, branch `cc/golive`, not merged; owner's go of 25 Oct 2026):** `LEGACY_LAUNCH` = `off`: the website side of S58. It pauses nothing on chain: the owner's `set_pause(launches = true)` and the revoke of every pending Approval stay owner actions (E3). They are not pre-merge items of the go-live: the pause is a recorded deviation, and there is no Approval to revoke (0 `Approval` accounts; section 11, go-live deploy checklist item 6). Rollback: `"on"`. |
| `COIN_V2_BATCH` (removed) | was off, on | Removed in S23 (this pull request, not merged). A switch from before v5 that was never on in production (wrangler.jsonc:109): the owner's daily round of approve, renew and revoke transactions on the live launchpad (`src/lpbatch.js`, PR #93), which master v5 forbids (no per-launch staff approval; `00-reconciliation.md` CV-9). Deleted with its line in `wrangler.jsonc`, `src/flags.js` `coinV2BatchOn`, `src/lpbatch.js`, the record step `batch`, the admin card and its styles, `LP.signAll`, `LP.canSignAll` and the wallet's `signAllTransactions` (only the round used them), the round's words in the founder's answers (with the review fixes also the phrase "at the owner's next approval round" and the round comments in `src/coinreview.js`, `src/coinready.js`, `src/cointakedown.js`, `src/lplaunch.js` and `public/dashboard-v2.js`, and the `nextRound` key of the dark review answer; finding S23-COPY-1), `docs/COIN-BATCH.md`, `test/lp-batch.test.js`, `test/lp-batch-ui.test.js` and `solana/tests-launchpad/22-batch-approvals.test.mjs`. Every reader took the production "off" branch. No module reads the name: a value left in the Worker's settings (`keep_vars` keeps the old "off" after the deploy that removes the line) changes no answer, and the round's routes answer 404 as they did with the switch off. The draft program's own batch launch approval (PR #83) is a different thing and is not touched. |

| `CC_HOURLY` | off, shadow, on | Hourly processing; "shadow" computes and publishes evidence and sends nothing |
| `PUBLIC_V5` | off, on | Public structure, `/architecture`, `/fees`, `/status`, `/api/status`, and the policy `master` key. Stage S07 (merged in PR #96, `3810c69`; off in production) built the policy part: the `master` key on `/api/policy` and `/api/policy/master`, counted only while the whole configuration check passes; the pages and `/api/status` follow in S52. |

The labels themselves (S48) are built in the rebrand stage (branch `cc/rebrand`; this pull request, not merged, switched off; section 11, "Rebrand stage"). With `BRAND=citycoin` in `wrangler.jsonc` the page build writes every page without "vicinity" in any case form and records `brand: "citycoin"` in `src/pages-built.js`. The Worker uses the Citycoin.dev words, and hides the legacy token (`src/official.js` `legacyHidden`), only while the deployed pages were built with that label set (`src/brand.js` `brandOf`), so a `BRAND` set only in the Cloudflare dashboard changes nothing. Since the rebrand review (F12), a Worker setting that differs from a Citycoin.dev build keeps the Citycoin.dev labels and the legacy token hidden (`BRAND_PAGES_MISMATCH`). The page build refuses `BRAND=citycoin` unless the same `wrangler.jsonc` sets `LEGACY_SEATS=frozen` (with its accepted `DEPLOYMENT_ENV`) and an `EMAIL_FROM` without the old name (`pageBrandPrerequisites`; review findings F3 and F7). A refused value is silent, as today (review note P3). **Go-live (this pull request, branch `cc/golive`, not merged; owner's go of 25 Oct 2026):** `BRAND` = `citycoin`, with `WALLET_ONLY=on` added to its build prerequisites, so that one edition of the Rules and Terms (version 7 and 2026-10-25, `src/editions.js`) goes with one sign-in. Rollback: `""`. |

The noindex rule of `public/_headers` moves from citycoin.dev to vicinity.city and www.vicinity.city, so the canonical site is indexed and the old host's copies are not. |

Validation refuses the switch without `MAIN_TOKEN_MINT` (`MAIN_TOKEN_MINT_MISSING`) and without `LEGACY_SEATS=frozen` (`MAIN_TOKEN_ON_WITH_LEGACY_SEATS`); in `src/masterpolicy.js` `FLAG_VALUES`. At run time it counts only through `src/v5policy.js` `mainTokenV5`: the deployed pages must have been built with it (`src/pages-built.js`, which the page build writes and which stays `false` until the label migration S48), `BRAND` must be that build's label set, and both configuration checks must accept the deployment. Otherwise the site is today's. |

## 6. Ordered stages, one pull request each

The phases follow master section 01 ("Ordered execution") and section 37 ("Migration order"). A stage may merge earlier than its position only when its dependencies allow it. The order in the list is the master's.

How section 37's migration order maps to the stages:

| Section 37 step | Stages |
|---|---|
| 1. Record branch and deployment state; validate backups | S01; backups in S08 and S55 |
| 2. Add and backfill the master schemas | S04, S08 (each group lazily, per switch) |
| 3. Map verified permanent founders and existing coins | S08, S16. Today there are 0 seats (GET `/api/seats`) and no listed city coin (GET `/official-coins.csv`), so nothing is expected to map. The census confirms it before cutover. |
| 4. Deploy compatible API reads with the old writers paused | S16, S23 |
| 5. Remove seven-day, rotation, Google and GPS-return gates; wallet-only in preview or testnet | S13, S15, S16 |
| 6. Reproducible holder snapshots; dry-run one-time selection | S17, S18 |
| 7. Rehearse custom launch, trade, pairs and hourly distributions with duplicate and recovery tests | S45 |
| 8. Build hashes, authority map, registry and policy versions, independent audit package | S38, S57, S59 |
| 9. Legacy new-launch disable plus cross-program duplicate checks | S35, S58 |
| 10. Deploy production flags disabled; enable only with passing evidence and real signing authority | S60 to S64 |

While `WALLET_ONLY` counts, `/api/signup/*` is the registration, whatever `SIGNUP_FLOW` says. Order on the server: start, location (today's checks; only the city is kept), Terms, an optional display name (`POST /api/signup/name`, the username rules), then the wallet. A `register` challenge is issued only once the city and the Terms are done (`409 location_required`, `409 terms_required`). Its text names the city and country, and it is bound to the sign-up and that city (ref `s:<sign-up>:<city>`). Three ways to the wallet: (1) this browser's wallet, `POST /api/signup/wallet`; (2) a register hand-off, `POST /api/signup/handoff`, `/info`, `/claim` and `/status`: one live 192-bit code per sign-up, five minutes (policy `identity.handoff_seconds`), only its hash stored, the city snapshot in `handoffs.bind`, bound to its first opener (`__Host-vlo`) and to the connection or, behind iCloud Private Relay, to the country (the wallet link's own rules, helpers exported unchanged from `src/walletlink.js`); (3) a register pairing, `POST /api/pair {purpose: "register"}`: five minutes, bound to the sign-up and city (`pairs.signup_id`, `pairs.bind`), approved by a phone's register challenge that carries the check number, finished by the computer. Codes travel only in POST bodies, and the links carry them in the fragment (`/connect#register=…`, `/connect#pair=…`), which no browser sends to a server or puts in a Referer. One D1 batch: the gate (the challenge, plus the hand-off or the pairing, used up only while the sign-up is alive with its Terms and that city), `INSERT INTO users … SELECT … FROM signups … ON CONFLICT(wallet) DO NOTHING`, a 30-day wallet session for `users WHERE wallet = ?`, then this browser's previous session, the sign-up, its pairings and its location hand-offs go. A lost race returns the existing account, its city unchanged (`existing: true`). The account records `home_city`, `home_name`, `home_country`, `terms_*`, `signup_completed_at`, `loc_verified_at`, `loc_policy` (`citycoin-master-v5`) and `loc_method` (`inside` or `nearest`); provider `wallet`, provider id the wallet. It never records a coordinate, and `loc_net` dies with the sign-up. An expired sign-up restarts registration (`401 no_signup` in the browser, `410 signup_expired` through a hand-off), never a login check. The connection must still match the location step (the old finish's `recheckLocation`; `403 location_unverified`, only the location is forgotten). Retired while the switch counts (404 before any method check): the account step (`/api/signup/email`, `/email/verify`, `/account/reset`), the old `/api/signup/finish` and the location hand-off into a wallet app (`/api/signup/location/handoff*`; a sign-up row's link answers `410 expired` at `/api/locate/handoff/complete`). Not built in this stage: the connect page (S12) and `legacy_state` (S14), both in this pull request. Not built: the backfill of existing members' new columns, `city_assignments` and the redundant partial unique index (planned for S08 or S15; S08, merged, left its `cc5-identity` group reserved and empty, which leaves S15; the column `UNIQUE` on `users.wallet` is the guard). Design text: Location before wallet, enforced on the server. A wallet-keyed atomic register batch (`ON CONFLICT(wallet)`; `users.wallet` is UNIQUE, src/store.js:55). Register hand-off (5 minutes; codes travel only in POST bodies) and register pairing. The old account routes and the in-wallet-app location hand-off answer 404. | `WALLET_ONLY` | **No change at merge** (switch unset; switches-off parity in section 11). ### Phase 6. WP-04 and WP-05: six-hour snapshots and permanent founder (website side first)

The on-chain FounderRecord is built with the program changes (S33). Permanent selection is switched on only after deployment, audit and observation (S61).

| Stage | Pull request | Switch | Live site | Checks | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **S16** Legacy seat freeze and old-rule copy removal | **This pull request, built on local branch `cc/s16`: server side, then the page and dashboard copy, then the review fixes (not merged; `LEGACY_SEATS` unset, switches-off parity in section 11).** Copy part: with `LEGACY_SEATS=frozen` in `wrangler.jsonc` the page build replaces every passage that presents the seven-day hold, the Stake Ladder, the Seed Steward, squads, founder windows, endorsements, objections or manager elections as current (55 exact passages in `scripts/pages/frozen-copy.mjs`, each applied exactly once or the build stops; the page sources stay as they are). The replacements are neutral: "Founder seats, squads and elections are closed for changes", seats, terms and results already on record stay as they are, a country manager's term runs until its end date and until a country has a manager the admin covers it; nothing describes a new selection. The dashboard, map, token, coin, rules and admin scripts read `legacySeats` from `/api/me` and then offer no claim, application, endorsement, vote, squad, objection decision, election or Resign, and show a seat, application, term or result only as on record (the admin's waiting count leaves out objections, which cannot be decided while frozen). The Terms of Use are not changed (owner text, E12) and the rules version stays 6. The gate `src/v5policy.js` `legacySeats` (see the `LEGACY_SEATS` switch row in section 5). Remove it" (`src/walletlink.js` `handleDisown`: the wallet, the other sessions and the codes still go, but no seat, squad or application row is written while frozen), and the unlink (`handleUnlink`: since review fix SEC-S16-2 a seat, an open application or a squad place no longer blocks it while frozen, because resign, withdraw and leave answer 410 and no window closes; the rows stay as recorded, each with its own wallet). The job's seats and elections steps answer `{ skipped: "legacy_seats_frozen" }` before any read; the balance sample is still taken (the Founding Supporters snapshot and the admin growth chart read it) but updates no streak, and `tenure` answers "no streak" while frozen. `canModerate`'s founder branch is off (`powersOf` adds `founderModeration: false`; `/api/mod` answers `moderator: false` to a founder alone); managers and admins moderate as before. Nothing was changed." No grace period starts and no seat is released after repeated graces (before the fix such a check wrote `status = 'grace'` or `'released'`, and the skipped job never put the seat back). Two layers, as the merged S08 schema has them (`city_metadata_versions`, `schema-and-api.md` 3.5). (1) Content v2, one per city version and the same for every quote (`contentV2`): the GeoNames city id, the list's "CC.ADM1" region id (or null for a place the list has no state or region for, which reads "No state or region."; an omitted region is refused, `region_missing`; nothing is made up), the ISO country code, the names, the list's attribution ("GeoNames (geonames.org), CC BY 4.0") and the SHA-256 of the city list file (the list version), normalization `cc-norm-1`, the coin's name and ticker, the descriptive text (never null: the approved text, or the live default's words `The city coin of <city>.`), the description template `cc-desc-1`, and the picture with its source (the generated art, template t2, or an owner-approved upload). Its canonical JSON (RFC 8785 restricted to null, booleans, safe integers, strings, arrays and plain objects: keys sorted, no whitespace, refused instead of dropped) is the row's `canonical_json`, and its SHA-256 is the `content_hash`; `contentFromJson` reads a stored one back only as the same bytes. (2) Per quote (`renderQuote`, `buildCoinMetadataV2`, `buildPerQuote`): `renderDescription(city, region, country, quote, { symbol, text })` writes, in this exact wording: `<lead> City: <city>. State or region: <region>. Country: <country>. Pair: $<TICKER> / <QUOTE>. The per-quote layer takes only a content that `contentV2` or `contentFromJson` made, and computes its hash itself. Three registry places whose list entry lacks a region that applies (Chongming, Chagni, Nouakchott) are refused with a null region until the owner reviews their region. Name and ticker rules are the program's (curve:programs/citycoin-curve/src/helpers.rs:39 and :112), with names also in `cc-norm-1`. S22's own registry does not know `t1c` and this module's header comment still says t1 only; the stage that wires S22's renderer registers `t1c` too: section 10, the coin art t2 note.) | Stage | Pull request | Switch | Live site | Checks | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **S42** Hourly engine in shadow | A sampler that takes one unannounced sample in each 15-minute segment plus the cutoff. Today's 10-minute cron (wrangler.jsonc:141) puts exactly one predictable tick in the 15-30 and 45-60 minute segments, so the sampler uses a 1-minute cron or a Durable Object alarm inside the same Worker, under the D1 lease. Exclusions: vaults, treasury, keeper, verifiers, publisher, team wallets and the permanent founder. Weight = min(cutoff, average) in exact integer math. Floored allocation with carry; hours with insufficient samples are carried. `hourly_actions` keyed by cluster, program, coin, quote mint, UTC hour, action and batch. Drift and missed-hour alerts. Shadow computes and publishes evidence and sends nothing. | `CC_HOURLY=shadow` | No change on mainnet (no custom-program coin exists) | Advances B07, B08, B09, D10 | S08, S36 (devnet) |
| **S43** Two hourly verifier services | Verifier A and verifier B are deployed and credentialed separately, with separate RPC providers. They must agree on cutoff, recipient-list hash, quote, funded amount and policy, then sign the 2-of-2 root. Disagreement means no root. | `CC_HOURLY=shadow` until they agree for the whole shadow window | No change | Advances B09 | S42; external E4 |
| **S44** Keeper | Hourly platform and founder payouts; buyback runs at least 150 slots apart under the depth cap; holder push batches; recipient-account creation from the keeper's operations wallet; bounded retries with backoff; receipts; no catch-up batching after a missed hour | `CC_HOURLY=on` only after shadow evidence, devnet first | No change on mainnet | **Closes B10**; advances B07, B08, B14 (keeper restart) | S42, S43; external E4, E8 |
| **S45** Devnet end-to-end rehearsal through the preview website | Deploy the S38 binary on devnet with throwaway keys. Commands and results go to `test-report.md`, `selection-proof.md`, `metadata-review.md` and `hourly-operations.md`. | `LAUNCH_V5`, `CC_TRADING`, `CC_HOURLY` on preview only | No change on production | **Closes A10, A11, A13, B01, B03, B04, B07, B08, B09, B11, M01, M02, M03, M04** (devnet evidence) | S05, S41, S44; external E1, E8 |

## 7. Status of the 50 acceptance checks

"Not started" means the check has not been run and none of its pass condition is verified. Until steps 1 to 3 merged, it also meant that no v5 work for the check was merged; now a stage in "Built in" marked "(merged)" is on main with its switches off (PR #95 to #98): part of the check is built, nothing of it is active, and the status stays "Not started" until the check is run at its "Closes at" stage. A stage marked "(this pull request)" is built in this pull request with its switches off and is not merged; the same rule applies. Two checks hold today, and the evidence is given in their rows. "Built in" lists the stages that implement the check. "Closes at" is the stage where the full pass condition is verified.

## 10. Decisions the master already made, and owner actions that stay real

**Owner amendment 01 (24 October 2026), after the master:** a new main token with a new mint, a new official X page and a new official Telegram, centrally configured and verified; `$VICINITY` is legacy with no automatic swap or migration. This is decided; only the values are pending (E18). The X address was supplied on 24 Oct 2026 (next paragraph); the mint and the Telegram address are still pending.

**Owner decisions of 24 October 2026, in writing, after the master and amendment 01:**

1. **The official X account:** "the twitter handle is @CityCoinDev", then the link "https://x.com/citycoindev". Configured as `OFFICIAL_X_URL` = `https://x.com/CityCoinDev` (the form the owner wrote first; X handles do not depend on letter case) by the configuration pull request from branch `cc/config-x`, not merged. The record (`amendment-01-new-main-token.md` section 4.4) has the shape check (step 2, done). Step 3 is open: there is only an automated HTTP read of the account ("CityCoin", `@CityCoinDev`, account number `2113657954452819968`), and no person has opened it in a browser. Step 4, proof that the account is the owner's, is open too, so the value rests on the owner's written statement and no switch may show it until the record is complete. With every master switch off, only `GET /api/admin/config` (admin only) reports that it is set.
2. **No forwarding from the old names:** "i dont want any domain forward from vicinty. leve that blank both. vicinity.city and vicinitycity.com i wil need them for other purpose." No 308, 301 or 302 from `vicinity.city`, `www.vicinity.city`, `vicinitycity.com` or `www.vicinitycity.com` to citycoin.dev; after the move both names are left blank (not served by this Worker, no redirect) for the owner's other use. This replaces master section 04's redirect of the old hosts and its 12-month window; `policy.json` records it (a second entry in `amendments`, and `release_gates.legacy_redirect_min_months` 0). In this plan: S56 is dropped; S55 makes citycoin.dev canonical and removes the vicinity.city routes with no redirect, only after the legacy launch path is closed (S58); vicinity.city keeps serving until citycoin.dev serves the site; the S03 redirect code stays merged and off until S65 removes it; the S04 host binding stays, for the time both hosts serve. The vicinitycity.com forward is the owner's own step (E16). Order, checks and what stops answering: `domain-cutover.md` sections 0, 3 and 4.

The rebrand stage applies it (section 11, "Rebrand stage"; branch `cc/rebrand`, not merged, switched off). With `BRAND=citycoin`, no page, script, Worker answer, e-mail or admin view shows "vicinity" in any case form, apart from the serving host until citycoin.dev serves the site. The legacy token is not shown, and nobody's `$VICINITY` holdings are swapped or touched. Before the switch, the owner must approve the Terms wording and decide on the Rules and Terms versions.

**Approved by the master; not open questions.** The 2.50% trade fee split 50/70/50/50/30 (liquidity, platform, founder, holders, buyback). Zero platform launch, send and receive fees. Wallet-only login. Location once. Six-hour largest-holder selection with one permanent founder per city. Owner-selected country managers. Owner-reviewed metadata changes. SOL, USDC and approved SPL quotes. Hourly automatic processing. The Civic Atlas theme. The master also settles the draft program's open owner decisions O11 (time lock), O12 (payout opt-in), O30 to O32 (batch approval), O35 (guardian configuration) and O36 (buyback rule) (curve:VICINITY-CURVE-DESIGN.md:3884-3910), in the directions given in stages S29 to S37.

**Delegated implementation choices, recorded here when made:** the legacy-link window length; switch names where they are still placeholders; the theme switch name; the exact versioned metadata path on citycoin.dev.

Recorded in stage S07 (merged in PR #96 as `3810c69`; switches off):
- The deployment environment is the Worker setting `DEPLOYMENT_ENV` (unset, test, preview, production). The policy requires an environment once any switch changes, and no existing setting carries one (`SITE_MODE` means the test-lab mode, not this).
- The deployment inputs without a Worker setting today (the cluster, the program, the nine role keys, the enabled quote configs and their chain facts) get no invented setting name: they read as unset, so a switch that needs one stays refused until the stage that builds it names the setting (schema-and-api.md section 2.2).
- "Keeps every dependent feature off while any error exists" applies in S07 to `PUBLIC_V5`: the public policy appears only while the whole configuration is accepted. Since the S02/S02A/S07 review it applies to `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` too (`src/v5policy.js` `mainTokenV5`): a refused `MAIN_TOKEN_MINT` or link, or any v5 configuration error, leaves the whole site as it is today instead of the S02A per-feature "not launched" fallbacks. Later switches are gated the same way in their own stages.
- `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` also needs the pages built for it. With `run_worker_first` as a list and `not_found_handling: "404-page"` (wrangler.jsonc), the Worker never sees a page, the 404 page included, so the footers, the /official groups and the client scripts' `$VICINITY` labels follow the switch only through the page build. The build writes `src/pages-built.js` (`mainTokenV5`, `brand`) from `wrangler.jsonc` and refuses the switch until the label migration (S48) and while the Worker would refuse its configuration; the Worker counts the switch only when that file says the pages carry it and `BRAND` is the same label set. A switch set only in the Cloudflare dashboard therefore changes nothing (one log line names why).
- While `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` counts, the legacy seat and election machinery, which measures `$VICINITY`, makes no new decision: the job's seats and elections steps do nothing and every seat, squad, endorsement, objection, resign and election writer answers 410 `seats_frozen` (the reads stay). This was the run-time half of `LEGACY_SEATS=frozen`; since S16 (server side, local branch `cc/s16`) both freezes are one gate, `src/v5policy.js` `legacySeats`, which `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` counting also turns to "frozen". So the answers with `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` counting are no longer those of `c5df86a` byte for byte (review note P3; not reachable today: `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` counts only with pages built for it, and the committed page record has `mainTokenV5: false`): the 410 message drops "while the main token changes", `/api/policy` and `/api/me` gain `legacySeats`, `founderMin` and `founderAmount` are dropped, founder moderation is off, badges, steps and squad offers change, and a founder action below the amount answers `below_amount` with no grace period.
- `SITE_ORIGIN` alone never moves the old-host redirect: www.vicinity.city, vicinitycity.net and www.vicinitycity.net keep forwarding to vicinity.city until `HOST_REDIRECT` (S03) introduces a configured target with its own check. An origin that `src/site-config.js` refuses is refused by the v5 configuration check too (and logged once), so an accepted configuration is one the Worker really runs with.
- The public main-token mint and official links appear only while `MAIN_TOKEN_V5` is on (the moment the site's consumers use them), never earlier; `main_token.verified` stays `null` until the verification record is machine-readable; `purchase_links` reads `"hidden"` or `"shown"` from the existing quote path.
- The `master` key is the public summary named by `policy.public_policy.exposed` plus the amendment-01 blocks; the full policy values are on `/api/policy/master` only, so `/api/policy` stays small.

Recorded in stage S14 (branch `cc/s09`; this pull request, not merged, switches off):
- `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL` is an ISO 8601 date or time (`YYYY-MM-DD`, or a time with `Z` or an offset). A bare date is 00:00 UTC of that day, so `2026-12-31` closes the window at the start of 31 December. The connect page shows the exact instant ("31 December 2026, 00:00 UTC" for that value), never a day that could be read as included. The window length itself (the date) is still chosen in S15.
- Once the scheduled job has marked the accounts still without a wallet `closed_unresolved` (S14 checklist item 4, section 11), the configuration removes `LEGACY_LINK` and `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL` together: the legacy routes become the S10 404 and the accounts stay `closed_unresolved` (no way in, data kept). The job marks accounts only while `LEGACY_LINK` is still on with the passed date (src/jobs.js:62 in this pull request's tree skips `src/legacy.js` `closeLegacyWindow` when the window is off), so removing both values before a job run after the end leaves those accounts `legacy_unlinked`. Keeping `LEGACY_LINK=on` with the passed date keeps the 410 answers instead; a passed date left alone in `wrangler.jsonc` stops the page build. Changed by the S09-S14 review (SEC-4): `LEGACY_LINK` off is no window, whatever `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL` says, and `closed_unresolved` is not final: a later window (`LEGACY_LINK=on` with a new `LEGACY_LINK_UNTIL` ahead) lets such an account log in and link a wallet again. Opening a later window is a new decision.
- A link ends the restricted session, and the dashboard opens only after a wallet sign-in (master section 05: "Do not restore Google as the new dashboard login path").
- No e-mail is sent after a link: today's "wallet connected" mail offers a "Remove it" that the window does not have.
- A phone's wallet links through a link pairing (the computer shows a code; the phone's wallet approves with a `legacy_link` challenge naming the check number). Today's carry routes are not reused for the window, and they answer 404 while `WALLET_ONLY` counts.
- The census is written once into `users.legacy_state` the first time the window counts (open or closed). Accounts made later by a wallet registration stay NULL: they are never a legacy case.

Recorded in stage S22 (local branch `cc/s22`; this pull request, not merged, nothing calls it):
- The exact versioned metadata path on citycoin.dev (a delegated choice listed above): `https://citycoin.dev/coin-meta/v1/<mint>.json`, the draft program's own prefix (curve:sdk/curve/constants.mjs:71 and curve:programs/citycoin-curve/src/constants.rs:217 at `77faf53`), and the pictures at `https://citycoin.dev/coin-meta/v1/art/<sha256>.png`, the legacy `/coin-meta/art/<sha256>.png` layout (src/coinart-store.js `artPath`) under the versioned prefix. S27 serves both; the legacy `/coin-meta/*` answers stay as they are.
- The description wording, template `cc-desc-1`. The master fixes the fields (city, state or region, country and the actual quote pair; a missing region rendered explicitly, never made up: sections 24 and 25, `policy.json` `metadata.description_must_include`) but writes no sentence. So the words are the site's own: the live default lead `The city coin of <city>.` (src/coinmeta.js, src/coindefaults.js), the field names of master section 24 ("City", "State or region", "Country"), the site's pair notation "Pair: $UTICA / SOL" (public/launchpad.js `pairText`) and the legacy JSON's closing sentence with the policy's product name and origin ("Launched on Citycoin.dev (https://citycoin.dev) on Solana."; src/coinmeta.js has it for Vicinity). A place with no state or region reads "No state or region." Other words are a new template name, never an edit of this one.
- One approved descriptive text serves every allowed quote (the pair selector changes only the pair field, section 25), so the text may not name a quote (SOL, WSOL, USDC, USDT, SOLANA or the selected quote's symbol, in any letter case; a place's own name, such as "Costa del Sol", does not count), a link, an address or markup ("<" and ">"). The pair can then only come from the mandatory field. Since the review (finding SEC-4) the checks also read a folded form of the text: compatibility forms (full-width and mathematical letters) to plain letters, no diacritics, a pinned set of look-alike letters (Cyrillic, Greek, Latin small capitals, Lisu) as Latin ones, then words, with a word spelled letter by letter ("S O L", "S.O.L.") and 0, 1 and 5 inside a word read as o, l and s; a link also with full-width, ideographic or leader dots and "[.]"; an address also when split by spaces into mixed chunks. What stays out of reach: other look-alikes (Unicode's full confusables list is not carried), a paraphrase ("the coin of the Solana chain" is caught, "paired with the dollar stablecoin" is not), a link without a dot. Those stay with the owner's review of every text (M03). The text is not limited to Latin and the city's own scripts (an option the review named): a local-language text in any script is allowed.
- Two layers, since the review (finding SF-1, high). The merged S08 schema (`schema-and-api.md` 3.5) keys a version by a pair-independent `content_hash` with UNIQUE (city_id, content_hash), keeps the approved text in `descriptive_text` (NOT NULL), and keeps the per-quote descriptions and metadata hashes in `per_quote_json`. S22 as first built hashed the selected quote and the pair-specific description into the content, so one version had a content hash per quote. Now the content (`canonical_json`, `content_hash`) holds no quote and no rendered description, and the default's text is stored as its lead `The city coin of <city>.`; the description, the metadata JSON and its hash (`meta_hash`, `metadata_json_sha256`) are per quote. The owner approves the content before any pair is chosen (section 25), and a launch binds the per-quote metadata hash with the quote config. `per_quote_json` is keyed by quote config id; the master policy has `config_id` 0 for SOL, while USDC's id is assigned with the quote configs (S31), so `perQuoteJson` refuses a quote without one. The content also names its list version: the SHA-256 of the city list file the place came from (`public/data/cities.json`), so a new list is a new content.
- The picture's source, since the review (finding SF-2): `{ sha256, source: "template", template: "t2" }` for the generated art, or `{ sha256, source: "upload" }` for an owner-approved upload (`city_metadata_uploads.normalized_sha256`, sections 25 and 40, M02 and M03). A golden vector covers an approved upload.
- `cc-norm-1` is pinned to Unicode 15.1, since the review (findings SF-3 and SEC-6). `String.prototype.normalize` uses the runtime's own Unicode data: an approved text with a combining mark that Unicode 16.0 added gave different bytes on Node 21 (Unicode 15.1) and Node 22 (16.0), so a stored version re-rendered on another runtime would have lost its `meta_hash`. Now every code point must be assigned in Unicode 15.1 (a committed table, `src/cc-unicode.js`, which `scripts/cc-unicode-table.mjs` writes on a Unicode 15.1 runtime), and Unicode's normalization stability policy then gives the same NFC bytes on 15.1 and every later version; an older runtime refuses (`runtime_unicode_too_old`) rather than give other bytes. The marks and the letters and digits the rules use come from the same table. `cc-norm-1` also refuses private-use code points, noncharacters, U+FFFC and U+FFFD, a combining mark at the start or after a space, and more than 4 marks in a row. Every city, region and country name of the list passes on Node 20, 21 and 22. These are edits of `cc-norm-1` before any coin carries it (nothing was merged or stored); a later change is a new version name.
- Open owner review (finding SF-4): three registry places have no region in the list although their country has one that applies: Chongming (CN, GeoNames 13608003; a district of Shanghai), Chagni (ET, 11048860; Amhara) and Nouakchott (MR, 2377450). Rendering "No state or region." for them would assert something false, so `contentV2` refuses them with a null region (`region_unreviewed`, `REGION_UNREVIEWED`) until the owner reviews their region; nothing is made up for them here. The other 26 registry places with no region in the list are city-states, territories or a disputed territory and render "No state or region.": Oranjestad, Noord and Santa Cruz (AW), Gustavia (BL), West Island (CC), Willemstad (CW), Flying Fish Cove (CX), Laayoune, Dakhla, Boujdour and El Marsa (EH, Western Sahara), Stanley (FK), Gibraltar (GI), Grytviken (GS), Hong Kong (HK), Marigot (MF), Macau (MO), Kingston (NF), Alofi (NU), Adamstown (PN), Singapore (SG), Philipsburg (SX), Providenciales and Cockburn Town (TC), Vatican City (VA) and Road Town (VG). The owner may want to look at the four Western Sahara places too.
- Only contents made here, since the review (finding SEC-3): `metadataJsonV2` and `renderQuote` accept only a content that `contentV2` or `contentFromJson` returned (deeply frozen and registered in the module) and compute its hash themselves, so S24 to S27 cannot pass a row from D1 straight into served metadata without it being built and checked again.
- Requirement for S27 (finding SEC-7): JSON does not escape "<", ">" or "&", so the hashed metadata may hold text that looks like markup (an approved text can no longer hold "<" or ">", but a place name could hold "&"). The permanent endpoints serve the JSON as `application/json` with `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` and never inline it into a page without escaping.
- No logo field in the new metadata JSON (the legacy JSON names `/brand/vicinity-512.png`): the Citycoin.dev brand files are not reviewed by the owner yet (`brand/README.md`), and a URL inside hashed metadata must be served for good. Adding one before the first launch is a new content version.
- Coin art t2 (`src/coinart-t2.js`) is on no coin, and its look is not reviewed by the owner. The owner's review of t2 is a gate before S41 draws the first new-program coin; a change after that review is t3. `src/coinart.js` keeps t1 as its only template and its default. (The rebrand stage, not merged, adds `t1c` there for the Citycoin.dev label set's ready-made coin, a distinct id because t2 is this different picture; t1 stays the default with the same bytes, and S22's t2 and t3 stay S22's: section 11, "Rebrand stage".) Wiring note for S24 to S27 and S41 (rebrand second check, RB-4): S22's registry (`src/coinart-t2.js` `ART_TEMPLATES`, keys t1 and t2) does not know `t1c`, and `artTemplate("t1c")` throws. A picture stored under `t1c` (possible only with `BRAND=citycoin` and `COIN_V2_READY=on`) would then have no template there, so the wiring must also register `t1c` (the same frozen object as `src/coinart.js` `TEMPLATES.t1c`) or resolve t1 and t1c through `src/coinart.js`. The header comment of `src/coinart-t2.js` ("src/coinart.js is not edited: its TEMPLATES still holds t1 only") is out of date since the rebrand branch; the file is S22's and the rebrand does not edit it.
- Alignment with PR #83 (`77faf53`): the same metadata prefix, name rule (1 to 32 bytes; no control, invisible or blank character; no space at an end or two in a row) and ticker rule (1 to 10 of A-Z and 0-9); `meta_hash` is the SHA-256 of the exact JSON served at the coin's URI (curve:CITYCOIN-CURVE-DESIGN.md:415); the image URL names the picture's SHA-256 (3.10). Divergences, none of which changes what the program accepts: (1) names are also NFC here (the program would take a decomposed name; the website never sends one); (2) PR #83 defines no field set or canonical form for the JSON ("Not built yet", curve:CITYCOIN-CURVE-DESIGN.md:4120-4123), so the field set (the legacy JSON's, plus the place ids, the attribution, the quote and the content hash) and the sorted-key RFC 8785 form are this stage's; (3) PR #83 at `77faf53` launches with SOL only (the daily batch list takes designs with the SOL pair, curve:CITYCOIN-CURVE-DESIGN.md:3020-3023), while S22 already renders one JSON per quote for SOL, USDC and any approved SPL quote; the quote configs reach the program in S31 and S32; (4) the JSON names the coin's mint (`external_url`, `website`), which the program derives from its id and the city id (curve:sdk/curve/pda.mjs `coinAccounts`), so it is known when the `meta_hash` is approved.

Recorded in stage S39 (local branch `cc/s22`; this pull request, not merged, no switch):
- Pinned, not removed. The master is silent on a swap fee: no line names Jupiter or a swap fee option. Section 29's zero-fee lines are about sends and receives ("SOL, main-token and supported-city-token sends/receives have zero Citycoin.dev platform fee. Builders contain no flat/percentage platform payment, hidden surcharge or transfer tax", master-v5.txt:1674-1675; "platform send fee = 0; platform receive fee = 0", :962), and the only line about an outside venue is "External third-party pools may not collect the custom-program fee; do not add a transfer tax" (:1667-1668). The pin follows B02's free-transfer intent and its "Decode launch and transfer transactions to prove zero platform collection" (:1682); the refusal follows from `keep_vars` (wording corrected in the review, finding SF-7; the code comment in src/cluster.js says the same). The plan allows either form (FE-2 in `00-reconciliation.md`, the S39 row). The fee-charging path is removed, so no swap build can carry a platform payment, and the two setting names are pinned to 0 with a refusal, because `wrangler.jsonc` sets `keep_vars: true` (wrangler.jsonc:20): a value set only in the Cloudflare dashboard outlives every deploy, and a removed option would ignore it in silence while whoever set it believes a fee is charged. The refusal makes such a value visible: a named reason in the answer, a log line, a preflight FAIL.
- A Worker has no start with settings in hand: the settings are read per request, as every switch here. The refusal is asked on the Jupiter path of each swap quote and build, and it is logged once per server (isolate) and reason. Since the review (finding SF-5) it comes after the body, the legacy-token and the curve checks: it was first asked before them, so a stray fee setting also stopped the city coins on their Meteora curve (`LAUNCHPAD_TRADING` is on in production, wrangler.jsonc:80), whose trade never carried a Jupiter fee. It never reaches another route, and within the two swap routes a bad body (400), a legacy token (409) and a curve coin (handed to `/api/launchpad/trade`) answer as without the setting. The attempt counters are written before it, as for any attempt; the per-wallet build counter is not. The full sweep with `SWAP_PLATFORM_FEE_BPS=50` and a fee account differs from the committed settings only in the 12 swap quote and build answers that go to Jupiter, the per-wallet build counter (not written), one log line and the Jupiter quote, build and price calls not made (section 11).
- Strict values: only unset, blank and "0" mean no fee ("00", "0.0" and "+0" are refused as malformed, not read as zero). Any `SWAP_FEE_ACCOUNT` is refused, even with the fee at 0: today's code charged nothing with an account alone (both settings were needed, src/cluster.js:93-97 at `edabab5`), so an account alone was always a stale or mistaken setting. Kept after the review (finding SEC-5, which offered a log-only warning for an account alone): the task asks for a refusal of any value other than 0, and a value that charged nothing at `edabab5` (an account alone, "abc", "0.5", "00" or a number above 1000) now stops the Jupiter swaps. That is a live change only if such a value sits in the dashboard, which is why the S39 deploy checklist (section 11, step 1) checks for it: the dashboard cannot be read from the release environment, so a live SOL to USDC quote before the merge and again right after the deploy stands in, with a revert if the second one answers `swap_unavailable`.
- While refused, `/api/swap/config` keeps `swap` as `SWAP` says and `platformFeeBps` 0, so the page opens its panel and the quote shows the page's existing `swap_unavailable` words; no static script changes. Kept after the review (finding U1, a nit, which suggested a refused flag in the config or hiding the Buy entry points): that would change a static script or the config answer, the case needs a stray dashboard fee setting, which checklist step 4 catches right after the deploy (S39 deploy checklist step 1), and since SF-5 the panel still trades the curve coins. The 503 answer names the reason (`platform_fee_not_zero`, `platform_fee_malformed` or `fee_account_set`) on purpose (finding SEC-9): it names the kind of setting, never its value, and it is the only place an operator without the logs sees why swaps stopped. The page's own wording for a fee above 0 (public/swap.js:1193, 1278) stays, unreachable.
- The Send line uses `src/brand.js`'s `productName`, the label the layout's "Vicinity never asks …" line uses. `src/brand.js` at `edabab5` holds today's label set only (any other `BRAND` is `BRAND_UNKNOWN`), and S39 adds none: the Citycoin.dev set belongs to the label migration (S48, branch `cc/rebrand`, with its own page-build check). Left as it is, because neither the master nor B02 asks for it in S39: the Fees section's sentence "No Vicinity fee on wallet transfers; only Solana's network fee and, for a first-time recipient, one-time account rent." (src/fees.js:172), which `cc/rebrand` renames. (Since the rebrand stage, branch `cc/rebrand`, not merged: the Send line takes the label set's words through `src/brandwords.js` `bw` (`planSend({ bw })`, the route passing `bw: brandWords(env)`), S39's `brand` parameter is gone, and `src/brand.js` holds the Citycoin.dev label set, so "until then no `BRAND` value names that set" no longer holds on that branch; the words are unchanged for every label set. `test/free-transfer.test.js` checks the route's line with the Citycoin.dev build stand-in too: section 11, "Rebrand stage".)
- Merging with `cc/rebrand` (head `cff446d`, read only). A trial merge (`git merge-tree` of a temporary commit object; no branch moved) adds exactly one conflict to the nine that `cc/rebrand` already has with `edabab5`: `src/walletsend.js`, three one-line hunks (the `planSend` parameters, the fee line and the route's `planSend` call). `cc/rebrand` makes the same line follow its label set through its `src/brandwords.js` (`` bw`No Vicinity fee. …` ``). Both sides give the same words for every label set ("Vicinity" with today's; the set's `productName` otherwise). To resolve, keep either side's three lines. If `cc/rebrand`'s are kept, the two `planSend` calls with `brand:` and the source check of the route in `test/free-transfer.test.js` take `bw:` instead. S39's import was placed so that it does not conflict.
- The swap's route (review finding SEC-1). The route is the swap's accounts past its fixed places: pools, vaults and their programs, which Jupiter chooses. The Worker cannot check them one by one (a pool's vault is a stranger's token account by design), so the earlier wording "nothing written but the taker's own accounts and the route Jupiter lists" claimed more than the check: Jupiter decides what the route lists. What bounds the route is the minimum output, `otherAmountThreshold`, which Jupiter's program enforces on chain: whatever the route does, the taker's output account gains at least that amount or the transaction fails, so a loss is bounded by the slippage the person chose (1 % by default, at most 50 %). That bound rests on Jupiter's program, not on the Worker. Since the review the validator also refuses what it can tell: a wrong event authority (both layouts); in `shared_accounts_route_v2`, a program authority that is not the one Jupiter derives for the swap's id or input and output accounts that are not that authority's own (the two recorded shared answers in `solana/sdk/launchpad/fixtures/` agree); and the taker's account of a watched mint (SOL's WSOL account, USDC, USDT, `$VICINITY`) in a route that does not trade that mint. Still out of reach: a stranger's writable account among the route's accounts, a changed route plan, and the taker's accounts of other mints. The Worker's validator is now stricter than the SDK's port in `solana/sdk/launchpad/jupiter.mts`, which this pull request does not touch.
- Token-2022 in the swap (review finding SEC-2), not fixed here: refusing Token-2022 mints would change live answers for such mints, which the panel routes today, and S39 is a no-visible-change stage. It is recorded as an open item next to B02 (section 7).

**Real actions that only the owner (or members) can take:** the signatures in E2 and E3, the addresses and keys in E4 and E7, every production review decision in E17, and in Cloudflare the deploy token's access to the citycoin.dev zone (E1) and the detaching of vicinitycity.com from the countdown Worker (E16). Approval of the master is not a signature, a deployment, an audit or an owner review event, and this report never counts it as one.

## 11. How to run the current code; tests run for this report

- From `package.json`: `npm run build` copies vendor assets, builds the pages and runs the tests (`node scripts/copy-assets.mjs && node scripts/pages/build.mjs && npm test`). `npm run dev` starts `wrangler dev`. `npm test` runs `node --test test/*.test.js`. The build writes files (for example `public/vendor/qrcode.js`, scripts/copy-assets.mjs:27, and the generated pages), so it was not run when this report was first written. The phase 1 independent check ran it afterward (table below). It wrote only files that git ignores or that it left byte-identical: `git status` listed no new or changed path outside `docs/citycoin-master/`, `public/brand/`, `src/masterpolicy.js` and `test/masterpolicy.test.js`. S01 (merged in PR #95) takes the phase 1 independent check's run below as its record; no pre-existing failure was found.
- No preview exists, so there are no preview instructions yet. S05 adds them.
- No screenshots exist yet. S59 collects them.
